## Company 1: Sierra

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| **Website** | https://sierra.ai |
| **Headquarters** | San Francisco, USA |
| **Founded** | 2023 |
| **Company Size** | scaleup |
| **Description** | Sierra builds a conversational AI platform that enables enterprises to create, deploy, and manage AI agents for customer experience across chat, voice, email, SMS, WhatsApp, and other channels. The platform combines a no-code agent builder, a developer SDK, analytics, and a data layer to power autonomous and human-assisted customer interactions. |
| **Primary Verticals** | Financial Services (BFSI), Healthcare & Life Sciences, Telecom & Media, Retail & E-commerce |
| **Contact Email** | not found |
| **Verification** | PASS |

### Agents

**1. Sierra Agent OS**
- **Tagline:** Build, deploy, and optimize AI agents that deliver empathetic, action-oriented customer experiences across every channel.
- **Description:** Sierra Agent OS is an enterprise platform for building and running conversational AI agents that handle customer interactions autonomously or alongside human representatives. It uses a "constellation of models" architecture that orchestrates multiple foundation models from providers like OpenAI, Anthropic, and Meta to handle different aspects of conversations. The platform includes Agent Studio for no-code agent building, an Agent SDK for developer-driven agent creation with composable skills and guardrails, a data layer called Agent Data Platform that gives agents persistent memory and access to enterprise systems like billing and CRM, and Live Assist which provides real-time AI guidance to human care representatives. Agents can be deployed as a single instance across chat, voice, email, SMS, WhatsApp, ChatGPT, and contact centers, with outcome-based pricing tied to value delivered.
- **Use Cases:** Autonomous customer support across omnichannel touchpoints (chat, phone, email, SMS); subscription management, renewals, and retention workflows for media and telecom companies; AI-assisted human agent workflows with real-time guidance, auto-drafted responses, and intelligent escalation; proactive customer engagement using persistent memory and enterprise data integration
- **Industries Supported:** Financial Services (BFSI), Healthcare & Life Sciences, Telecom & Media, Retail & E-commerce
- **Business Functions Supported:** Customer Experience, Sales & Marketing, IT Operations, Data & Analytics, Operations & Supply Chain
- **Expected Business Outcomes:** ~90% CSAT scores for customer interactions; handling millions of customer inquiries per month at scale; faster resolution times and reduced contact center costs through autonomous agent handling and intelligent human-agent collaboration
- **Integrations:** CRM systems, billing platforms, inventory systems, policy databases, transaction systems, contact center platforms, ChatGPT, WhatsApp, SMS gateways, email systems
- **Source:** https://sierra.ai/product

## Company 2: Decagon

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| **Website** | https://decagon.ai |
| **Headquarters** | San Francisco, USA |
| **Founded** | 2023 |
| **Company Size** | scaleup |
| **Description** | Decagon is a conversational AI platform that enables enterprises to build, manage, and scale AI agents for customer experience across voice, chat, and email channels. The platform uses proprietary Agent Operating Procedures (AOPs) that let teams define agent behavior in natural language, which automatically compiles into executable code for handling complex customer interactions. |
| **Primary Verticals** | Retail & E-commerce, Financial Services (BFSI), Healthcare & Life Sciences, Telecom & Media |
| **Contact Email** | support@decagon.ai |
| **Verification** | PASS |

### Agents

**1. Decagon AI Agent Platform**
- **Tagline:** Enterprise-grade conversational AI that resolves millions of customer inquiries across every channel, 24/7.
- **Description:** Decagon AI Agent Platform is a unified, AI-native system for building, testing, and deploying autonomous customer experience agents across chat, email, and voice. At its core are Agent Operating Procedures (AOPs), which allow CX teams to define agent workflows in natural language that automatically compile into production-grade code, combining the flexibility of plain English with the precision of software. The platform includes built-in testing and QA with simulated conversations, live A/B experimentation for conversation routing, and Watchtower for real-time quality monitoring and alerting. It supports multi-language resolution and maintains brand consistency through enterprise-grade guardrails for handling sensitive operations like refunds and account changes.
- **Use Cases:** Automating tier-1 customer support resolution across chat, email, and voice; enabling self-service for account access recovery, returns processing, and charge disputes; providing AI-powered agent assist that gives human agents real-time response suggestions and knowledge-base lookups; running A/B experiments to optimize AI agent performance against different customer segments
- **Industries Supported:** Retail & E-commerce, Financial Services (BFSI), Healthcare & Life Sciences, Telecom & Media
- **Business Functions Supported:** Customer Experience, IT Operations, Data & Analytics, Operations & Supply Chain
- **Expected Business Outcomes:** Improved resolution rates (Notion reported a 34% improvement in resolution rate); reduced manual overhead for support teams through 24/7 automated handling of customer inquiries
- **Integrations:** Salesforce, Zendesk, Intercom, Kustomer, Confluence, Contentful, Amazon Connect, RingCentral, Zendesk Sunshine, SIP trunking, MCP (Model Context Protocol), custom APIs
- **Source:** https://decagon.ai/product/overview

**2. Decagon Voice**
- **Tagline:** Hyper-realistic AI voice agents that deliver human-like phone support at enterprise scale.
- **Description:** Decagon Voice is an AI-powered voice agent solution that handles inbound customer calls using lifelike, emotionally resonant speech synthesis. Voice agents operate through the same Agent Operating Procedures (AOPs) framework as Decagon's chat and email agents, ensuring consistent intelligence and behavior across all channels. The agents can access internal knowledge bases and applications in real time, execute external tool calls for dynamic data retrieval, and seamlessly hand off complex inquiries to human agents when necessary. It integrates with existing call center infrastructure via SIP trunking and CCaaS connectors, enabling deployment without replacing telephony systems.
- **Use Cases:** Handling inbound customer calls for account access recovery, returns, and billing disputes; providing 24/7 phone support without staffing constraints; augmenting contact centers during peak call volumes; delivering consistent voice-based support in industries where phone remains the preferred channel
- **Industries Supported:** Retail & E-commerce, Financial Services (BFSI), Healthcare & Life Sciences, Telecom & Media, Logistics & Transportation
- **Business Functions Supported:** Customer Experience, Operations & Supply Chain
- **Expected Business Outcomes:** Reduced call center staffing costs through automated inbound call handling; improved customer satisfaction via consistent, always-available phone support with natural-sounding voice interactions
- **Integrations:** Amazon Connect, RingCentral, SIP trunking, ElevenLabs (voice synthesis)
- **Source:** https://decagon.ai/blog/decagon-voice

**3. Proactive Agents**
- **Tagline:** AI concierge that anticipates customer needs and initiates outreach at the right time, for the right reason.
- **Description:** Proactive Agents is Decagon's next-generation agentic capability that shifts customer engagement from reactive support to proactive outreach. The system includes an outbound voice capability with voice models fine-tuned for the unique technical challenges of outbound calling, enabling AI agents to proactively contact customers without manual overhead. It features a user memory system that captures conversational context, preferences, sentiment signals, and behavioral patterns across interactions, using this accumulated intelligence to personalize future engagements. Proactive Agents can anticipate customer needs, remember individual nuances, and initiate contact through the appropriate channel at contextually optimal moments.
- **Use Cases:** Proactively reaching out to customers about order issues, delivery updates, or service disruptions before they contact support; re-engaging customers based on behavioral signals and past interaction history; automating outbound follow-ups for appointment confirmations, renewals, and service check-ins; delivering personalized concierge experiences at scale
- **Industries Supported:** Retail & E-commerce, Healthcare & Life Sciences, Logistics & Transportation, Automotive & Mobility
- **Business Functions Supported:** Customer Experience, Sales & Marketing, Operations & Supply Chain
- **Expected Business Outcomes:** Shift from reactive to proactive customer engagement, reducing inbound support volume; increased customer retention through personalized, anticipatory outreach
- **Integrations:** Decagon Voice, Decagon Chat, Decagon Email (unified platform), existing CRM and helpdesk connectors
- **Source:** https://www.businesswire.com/news/home/20260307486723/en/Decagon-Unveils-Proactive-Agents-The-AI-Concierge-for-Every-Customer-In-The-Agentic-Commerce-Era

**4. AOP Copilot**
- **Tagline:** Turn any prompt or SOP into a production-ready AI agent workflow in seconds.
- **Description:** AOP Copilot is an AI assistant embedded within the Decagon platform that generates production-ready Agent Operating Procedures from natural language prompts. It understands Decagon's complete library of workflow step types and their connections, enabling it to assemble validated, properly-structured AOPs from either detailed standard operating procedures or rough conceptual ideas. Generated workflows include built-in validation for structural soundness and consistently apply best practices for clarity, modularity, and performance. The tool eliminates the manual effort of translating business processes into agent logic, accelerating the journey from documentation to deployment.
- **Use Cases:** Rapidly converting existing SOPs and runbooks into executable AI agent workflows; enabling non-technical CX teams to build and iterate agent logic without engineering support; scaling AI agent coverage across new use cases by reducing workflow creation time; maintaining consistent quality standards across large libraries of agent procedures
- **Industries Supported:** Retail & E-commerce, Financial Services (BFSI), Healthcare & Life Sciences, Telecom & Media
- **Business Functions Supported:** Customer Experience, IT Operations, Engineering & DevOps
- **Expected Business Outcomes:** Dramatically reduced time from workflow idea to production deployment; expanded AI agent coverage without proportional increase in engineering resources
- **Integrations:** Native to Decagon platform (works with all Decagon integrations)
- **Source:** https://decagon.ai/resources/aop-copilot

- **Tagline:** A benchmark that evaluates whether AI agents can complete long-horizon, cross-application professional tasks in investment banking, consulting, and corporate law.
- **Description:** APEX-Agents evaluates agentic AI systems on realistic, multi-step professional workflows rather than single-turn prompts. It comprises 33 data-rich simulated work environments with 480 tasks requiring agents to navigate complex file systems, work across documents, spreadsheets, PDFs, chat, email, and calendar. The benchmark measures Pass@1 — probability of success on the first attempt. The entire dataset is open-sourced on Hugging Face, along with the Archipelago Docker-based evaluation harness on GitHub.
- **Use Cases:** Evaluating enterprise AI agent readiness; benchmarking agentic systems on long-horizon workflows; testing agent reliability under realistic conditions; guiding RL training strategies
- **Industries Supported:** Financial Services (BFSI)
- **Business Functions Supported:** Data & Analytics, Legal & Compliance, Finance & Accounting
- **Expected Business Outcomes:** Standardized agent evaluation before deployment; identification of failure modes; improved agent training
- **Integrations:** Hugging Face, GitHub (Archipelago), Docker
- **Source:** https://www.mercor.com/blog/introducing-apex-agents/

- **Use Cases:** AI video generation; consistent character creation across shots; cinematic pre-visualization; marketing video production
- **Industries Supported:** Telecom & Media, Retail & E-commerce, Education
- **Business Functions Supported:** Sales & Marketing, Customer Experience
- **Expected Business Outcomes:** Reduced video production time and cost; consistent brand visuals; rapid creative iteration; democratized cinematic-quality production
- **Integrations:** Runway API, Runway Workflows, MCP Server, ElevenLabs Dubbing API, enterprise SFTP, webhooks, SSO
- **Source:** https://runwayml.com/research/introducing-runway-gen-4

**2. Aleph**
- **Tagline:** An in-context AI video editing model that transforms existing footage through natural language instructions.
- **Description:** Aleph understands and transforms real footage based on text prompts rather than generating new content. Users upload video and describe edits like changing time of day, removing objects, adding effects, relighting, or generating new camera angles. It reconstructs 3D geometry to maintain temporal consistency and supports transparent backgrounds for compositing.
- **Use Cases:** Post-production editing via text prompts; object removal/addition; environmental transformation; scene relighting; generating alternate camera angles
- **Industries Supported:** Telecom & Media, Retail & E-commerce
- **Business Functions Supported:** Sales & Marketing, Customer Experience

- **Integrations:** Runway platform, Runway API, Runway Workflows, alpha channel export
- **Source:** https://runwayml.com/research/introducing-runway-aleph

**3. GWM-1**
- **Tagline:** A real-time, interactive General World Model that simulates reality for immersive worlds, robotics, and conversational avatars.
- **Description:** GWM-1 generates frame-by-frame simulations with physics, geometry, and lighting at 24fps. Three variants: GWM-Worlds creates explorable 3D environments from text/images; GWM-Robotics generates synthetic training data for robots; GWM-Avatars produces photorealistic conversational characters with lip sync and gestures. Supports interactive control through camera pose, robot commands, and audio.
- **Use Cases:** Real-time world generation for games; synthetic data for robotics training; interactive avatar creation; virtual environment prototyping
- **Industries Supported:** Telecom & Media, Manufacturing, Automotive & Mobility, Education
- **Business Functions Supported:** Engineering & DevOps, Operations & Supply Chain, Customer Experience
- **Expected Business Outcomes:** Reduced cost of building 3D environments; accelerated robotics training; interactive world exploration without manual design
- **Integrations:** Runway API, GWM-Robotics SDK, Runway platform
- **Source:** https://runwayml.com/research/introducing-runway-gwm-1

**4. Act-Two**
- **Tagline:** AI-powered motion capture that transfers full-body performances onto any character from a single video and reference image.
- **Description:** Act-Two captures head movements, facial expressions, upper body gestures, and hand tracking from a performance video and transfers them onto AI-generated or reference characters. Requires only a video and character reference — no motion-capture equipment, rigging, or manual animation. Supports voice modification and audio rendering alongside visual capture.
- **Use Cases:** Character animation from webcam video; performance-driven avatar creation; film pre-visualization; marketing content with animated characters
- **Industries Supported:** Telecom & Media, Retail & E-commerce, Education
- **Business Functions Supported:** Sales & Marketing, Customer Experience
- **Expected Business Outcomes:** Eliminated need for motion-capture studios; democratized Hollywood-quality animation; faster animated content production
- **Integrations:** Runway platform, Runway API, iOS app, voice modification tools
- **Source:** https://help.runwayml.com/hc/en-us/articles/42311337895827-Creating-with-Act-Two

**5. Runway Characters**
- **Tagline:** Real-time intelligent avatars that converse, act, and execute tasks across any visual style from a single image.
- **Description:** Runway Characters generates interactive, intelligent avatars from a single reference image across photorealistic, animated, or stylized styles with zero fine-tuning. Characters maintain natural expressions, eye movements, lip-syncing, and gestures during extended conversations. The API enables full customization of voice, personality, knowledge base, and executable actions, allowing avatars to create tickets, process orders, and pull from knowledge bases in real-time video interactions.

- **Integrations:** Runway API, enterprise knowledge bases, order management systems, ticketing systems
